# Insight bake-off: Leading software factories and agent-platform architectures (2026-11-11)

Run `sf-20261111`: 13 arms x 24 questions. Extractor claude-code / claude-sonnet-5-5; judge claude-code / claude-opus-5-5; seed 20261111; comparable key `cf35c94469fecf92`. [Interactive dashboard](dashboard.html) | [summary.json](summary.json)

## Read this first

- One run on one day: n = 24 questions at most; treat small gaps as noise.
- The extractor and judges are models; verdicts and values were not checked by hand.
- Strict credit: an arm earns a finding only if its own cited page supports it.
- 25 profile fields are unverified against vendor docs; see the profile table.
- 6 arms ran on a subset of questions (brave.research: 9, exa.research: 9, firecrawl.research: 9, parallel.research: 9, perplexity.research: 9, tavily.research: 9). Their totals cover only those questions; compare per question, and the significance tests use only questions both arms ran.
- claude-native.agent is imported: freeform research brief mapped to questions afterwards; not posed each question verbatim; 49 WebSearch + 48 WebFetch calls; cost not metered.

Pooled: 1787 insights of 3596 claim clusters, weighted 3225. Shares overlap, because a shared insight counts for every arm that earned it.

**Imported arms** were produced outside this run. Their claims were re-extracted and verified like every other arm's, but they did not run under the run's profiles, date or ledger.

- `claude-native.agent` (imported): Claude Code native WebSearch/WebFetch (Opus 5.5 research agent). Caveat: freeform research brief mapped to questions afterwards; not posed each question verbatim; 49 WebSearch + 48 WebFetch calls; cost not metered. Cost unknown; source sha256 `99802aeff03b5105`.

## Unique insights

What exactly one arm found and its own page supported.

| Value | Arm | Question | Finding | Source | Raw |
| ---: | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 3 | perplexity.research | Q01 | StrongDM's agents rewrote code to pass narrow tests, so the team moved its checks into scenarios kept outside the codebase, like a holdout set in model training. | [page](https://www.signadot.com/software-factory/) | [cell](data/cells/Q01/perplexity.research.json), [verify](data/grading/verify/22669bf4c7fbf975cc30.json), captured page (local only) |
| 3 | perplexity.research | Q01 | A swarm of agent testers simulating end users reportedly cost StrongDM about $10,000 a day in tokens. | [page](https://github.com/lago-morph/software-factory/blob/main/research/05-simon-willison.md) | [cell](data/cells/Q01/perplexity.research.json), [verify](data/grading/verify/6ef2aaed569879808a87.json), captured page (local only) |
| 3 | tavily.research | Q01 | Hacker News critics noted zero published benchmarks, defect rates, cost comparisons or production outcomes, and argued agent-written scenarios are still agent-written tests with shared blind spots between generator and discriminator agents. | [page](https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=46924426) | [cell](data/cells/Q01/tavily.research.json), [verify](data/grading/verify/8282baf1e46ef0950bfd.json), captured page (local only) |
| 3 | tavily.research | Q01 | Attractor saves a checkpoint to {logs_root}/checkpoint.json after each node, and on resume the first node degrades from full to summary:high fidelity because in-memory LLM sessions cannot be serialized. | [page](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/strongdm/attractor/main/attractor-spec.md) | [cell](data/cells/Q01/tavily.research.json), [verify](data/grading/verify/2c649205c36d6a3b6ae8.json), captured page (local only) |
| 3 | exa.research | Q01 | CXDB stores agent turn histories as a DAG with shared prefixes, O(1) branching via a new head pointer, content-addressed blob deduplication and a dynamic type registry. | [page](https://factory.strongdm.ai/products/cxdb) | [cell](data/cells/Q01/exa.research.json), [verify](data/grading/verify/d93e59de6704b6a43e0d.json), captured page (local only) |
| 3 | exa.research | Q01 | The Coding Agent Loop spec filters sensitive environment-variable names by default but calls this 'a safety default, not a security boundary,' since agents can still read variables via shell commands. | [page](https://github.com/strongdm/attractor/blob/main/coding-agent-loop-spec.md) | [cell](data/cells/Q01/exa.research.json), [verify](data/grading/verify/f1bb5573d25d585508bf.json), captured page (local only) |
| 3 | exa.search | Q01 | Dbggr reports StrongDM is the only company found stating at a primary source that it currently ships code no human reviews, yet it publishes no defect, incident, customer outcome, satisfaction score, usage or token-bill data. | [page](https://dbggr.dev/articles/software-factories/) | [cell](data/cells/Q01/exa.search.json), [verify](data/grading/verify/412c7c4533a580992113.json), captured page (local only) |
| 3 | exa.search | Q01 | In ImpossibleBench, model-based monitors caught 86-89% of cheating on simple coding tasks but only 42-65% on realistic repository tasks, suggesting model judges can miss model cheating. | [page](https://dbggr.dev/articles/software-factories/) | [cell](data/cells/Q01/exa.search.json), [verify](data/grading/verify/412c7c4533a580992113.json), captured page (local only) |
| 3 | exa.search | Q01 | Dex Horthy of HumanLayer reported his team's lights-off factory approach failed (site down in July 2025, rewrite from scratch by November) because passing tests carries no penalty for eroding maintainability, though he admits he cannot prove this and no benchmarks exist for it. | [page](https://dbggr.dev/articles/software-factories/) | [cell](data/cells/Q01/exa.search.json), [verify](data/grading/verify/412c7c4533a580992113.json), captured page (local only) |
| 3 | firecrawl.research | Q01 | StrongDM says its agents initially learned that `return true` could pass narrowly written tests while doing nothing useful. It says ordinary unit, integration, regression, end-to-end and behavior tests were insufficient alone, which motivated external scenarios, broader behavioral validation and DTU. | [page](https://factory.strongdm.ai/) | [cell](data/cells/Q01/firecrawl.research.json), [verify](data/grading/verify/6e7fbc41240b9d77e5a4.json), captured page (local only) |
| 3 | firecrawl.search | Q01 | StrongDM's scenarios describe step by step how a real customer would use the software and are hidden from the agents, so the question becomes how often the software would do what a real person needs rather than whether it passes. | [page](https://law.stanford.edu/2026/02/08/built-by-agents-tested-by-agents-trusted-by-whom/) | [cell](data/cells/Q01/firecrawl.search.json), [verify](data/grading/verify/c6067528590ec9979e14.json), captured page (local only) |
| 3 | firecrawl.search | Q01 | Per one account, a version ships when its satisfaction score clears a pre-defined threshold, and a drop such as 93% to 91% can be traced to specific scenarios and fed back into the next generation cycle. | [page](https://www.groktop.us/dark-factory/) | [cell](data/cells/Q01/firecrawl.search.json), [verify](data/grading/verify/57a562de51f2132e7259.json), captured page (local only) |
